/* ── TABS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function switchTab(id, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('panel-' + id).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('active');
  var t = document.getElementById('topbar-title');
  if (t) t.textContent = btn.getAttribute('data-title') || btn.textContent.trim();
  var m = document.querySelector('main');
  if (m) m.scrollTop = 0;
}

/* ── MODAL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function openModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open'); }
function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open'); }
document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(o => {
  o.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === o) o.classList.remove('open'); });
});

/* ── COPY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function copyEl(id) {
  const text = document.getElementById(id).innerText;
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
    .then(() => flashToast('Copied to clipboard.'))
    .catch(() => flashToast('Could not copy — your browser blocked clipboard access.'));
}
function copyText(text) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
    .then(() => flashToast('Copied to clipboard.'))
    .catch(() => flashToast('Could not copy — your browser blocked clipboard access.'));
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CRYPTO — pure Web Crypto API + Ed25519 via noble/ed25519 polyfill
   We implement Ed25519 manually using SubtleCrypto where available,
   falling back to a minimal pure-JS implementation.
   ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Base64URL helpers */
function b64uEncode(buf) {
  const bytes = buf instanceof Uint8Array ? buf : new Uint8Array(buf);
  let s = '';
  for (const b of bytes) s += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return btoa(s).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, '');
}
function b64uDecode(s) {
  s = s.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
  while (s.length % 4) s += '=';
  const raw = atob(s);
  const out = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) out[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

/* SHA-256 — from-scratch (PBSHA256), not WebCrypto. Kept async: every call
   site already awaits this, and no behavior changes for them. */
async function sha256(data) {
  if (!(data instanceof Uint8Array)) data = new TextEncoder().encode(data);
  return PBSHA256.sha256(data);
}

/* SHA-256 hex */
async function sha256hex(data) {
  const h = await sha256(data);
  return Array.from(h).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
}

/* Deterministic JSON for signing */
function canonicalJSON(obj) {
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) return '[' + obj.map(canonicalJSON).join(',') + ']';
  if (obj !== null && typeof obj === 'object') {
    const keys = Object.keys(obj).sort();
    return '{' + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + canonicalJSON(obj[k])).join(',') + '}';
  }
  return JSON.stringify(obj);
}

/* Merkle tree (RFC 6962 style) */
async function merkleRoot(leaves) {
  if (leaves.length === 0) return new Uint8Array(32);
  const hashes = await Promise.all(leaves.map(async l => {
    const data = new Uint8Array([0x00, ...new TextEncoder().encode(
      typeof l === 'string' ? l : JSON.stringify(l)
    )]);
    return sha256(data);
  }));
  let level = hashes;
  while (level.length > 1) {
    const next = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < level.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < level.length) {
        const combined = new Uint8Array([0x01, ...level[i], ...level[i+1]]);
        next.push(await sha256(combined));
      } else {
        next.push(level[i]);
      }
    }
    level = next;
  }
  return level[0];
}

/* Ed25519 keygen — from-scratch engine (v0.10). Seed randomness from the platform CSPRNG;
   all curve and hash math is ours. */
async function ed25519Generate() {
  const seed = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
  const pubRaw = PBED25519.pubFromSeed(seed);
  return { seed, pubRaw, privKey: null, pubKey: null };
}

async function ed25519Sign(message, privKeyOrSeed) {
  const seed = privKeyOrSeed instanceof Uint8Array ? privKeyOrSeed : new Uint8Array(privKeyOrSeed);
  return PBED25519.sign(message, seed);
}

async function ed25519Verify(message, signature, pubKeyOrBytes) {
  try {
    const pub = pubKeyOrBytes instanceof Uint8Array ? pubKeyOrBytes : new Uint8Array(pubKeyOrBytes);
    return PBED25519.verify(new Uint8Array(signature), message, pub);
  } catch { return false; }
}

function buildPkcs8(seed32) {
  // ASN.1 PKCS#8 wrapper for Ed25519 seed

const PB_SIGS = [
  { id:'Ed25519',      type:'classical',    secBits:128, engine:'webcrypto', standard:'RFC 8032' },
  { id:'ECDSA-P256',   type:'classical',    secBits:128, engine:'webcrypto', curve:'P-256', hash:'SHA-256', standard:'FIPS 186-5' },
  { id:'ECDSA-P384',   type:'classical',    secBits:192, engine:'webcrypto', curve:'P-384', hash:'SHA-384', standard:'FIPS 186-5' },
  { id:'ECDSA-P521',   type:'classical',    secBits:256, engine:'webcrypto', curve:'P-521', hash:'SHA-512', standard:'FIPS 186-5' },
  { id:'RSA-PSS-2048', type:'classical',    secBits:112, engine:'webcrypto', modulus:2048, standard:'RFC 8017' },
  { id:'RSA-PSS-3072', type:'classical',    secBits:128, engine:'webcrypto', modulus:3072, standard:'RFC 8017' },
  { id:'RSA-PSS-4096', type:'classical',    secBits:140, engine:'webcrypto', modulus:4096, standard:'RFC 8017' },
  { id:'Dilithium2',   type:'post-quantum', secBits:128, engine:'noble', pbx:'ml_dsa44',          standard:'FIPS 204 (ML-DSA-44)' },
  { id:'Falcon512',    type:'post-quantum', secBits:128, engine:'noble', pbx:'falcon512',         standard:'FN-DSA draft (Falcon-512)' },
  { id:'SPHINCS+-256', type:'post-quantum', secBits:128, engine:'noble', pbx:'slh_dsa_sha2_128f', standard:'FIPS 205 (SLH-DSA-SHA2-128f)',
    note:'Display name "SPHINCS+-256" is retained for UI continuity; actual parameter set is SLH-DSA-SHA2-128f (128-bit security, not 256). See defect record.' },
];
function sigInfo(id) { return PB_SIGS.find(s => s.id === id); }

async function sigKeygen(algId) {
  const info = sigInfo(algId);
  if (!info) throw new Error('unknown signature algorithm: ' + algId);
  if (algId === 'Ed25519') {
    const kp = await ed25519Generate();
    return { alg: algId, priv: b64uEncode(kp.seed), pub: b64uEncode(kp.pubRaw) };
  }
  if (info.curve) {
    const kp = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name:'ECDSA', namedCurve: info.curve }, true, ['sign','verify']);
    const priv = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey('pkcs8', kp.privateKey));
    const pub  = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey('raw', kp.publicKey));
    return { alg: algId, priv: b64uEncode(priv), pub: b64uEncode(pub) };
  }
  if (info.modulus) {
    const kp = await crypto.subtle.generateKey(
      { name:'RSA-PSS', modulusLength: info.modulus, publicExponent: new Uint8Array([1,0,1]), hash:'SHA-256' },
      true, ['sign','verify']);
    const priv = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey('pkcs8', kp.privateKey));
    const pub  = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey('spki', kp.publicKey));
    return { alg: algId, priv: b64uEncode(priv), pub: b64uEncode(pub) };
  }
  if (info.pbx) {
    const kp = PBX[info.pbx].keygen();
    return { alg: algId, priv: b64uEncode(kp.secretKey), pub: b64uEncode(kp.publicKey) };
  }
  throw new Error('unroutable algorithm: ' + algId);
}

async function sigSign(algId, msgBytes, privB64u) {
  const info = sigInfo(algId);
  if (!info) throw new Error('unknown signature algorithm: ' + algId);
  const priv = b64uDecode(privB64u);
  if (algId === 'Ed25519') return ed25519Sign(msgBytes, priv);
  if (info.curve) {
    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('pkcs8', priv, { name:'ECDSA', namedCurve: info.curve }, false, ['sign']);
    return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign({ name:'ECDSA', hash: info.hash }, key, msgBytes));
  }
  if (info.modulus) {
    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('pkcs8', priv, { name:'RSA-PSS', hash:'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']);
    return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign({ name:'RSA-PSS', saltLength:32 }, key, msgBytes));
  }
  if (info.pbx) return PBX[info.pbx].sign(msgBytes, priv);
  throw new Error('unroutable algorithm: ' + algId);
}

async function sigVerify(algId, msgBytes, sigBytes, pubB64u) {
  const info = sigInfo(algId);
  if (!info) return false;
  try {
    const pub = b64uDecode(pubB64u);
    if (algId === 'Ed25519') return await ed25519Verify(msgBytes, sigBytes, pub);
    if (info.curve) {
      const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', pub, { name:'ECDSA', namedCurve: info.curve }, false, ['verify']);
      return await crypto.subtle.verify({ name:'ECDSA', hash: info.hash }, key, sigBytes, msgBytes);
    }
    if (info.modulus) {
      const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('spki', pub, { name:'RSA-PSS', hash:'SHA-256' }, false, ['verify']);
      return await crypto.subtle.verify({ name:'RSA-PSS', saltLength:32 }, key, sigBytes, msgBytes);
    }
    if (info.pbx) return PBX[info.pbx].verify(sigBytes, msgBytes, pub);
  } catch { return false; }
  return false;
}

/* Runtime capability probe (fast; keygen for EC/Ed only, RSA/PQ assumed if constructor present) */
async function probeCapabilities() {
  const caps = {};
  for (const d of PB_DIGESTS) {
    try { await digestBytes(d.id, utf8('probe')); caps[d.id] = true; }
    catch { caps[d.id] = false; }
  }
  for (const s of PB_SIGS) {
    if (s.engine === 'noble') { caps[s.id] = !!(window.PBX && PBX[s.pbx]); continue; }
    if (s.id === 'Ed25519') {
      try { await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name:'Ed25519' }, true, ['sign','verify']); caps[s.id] = true; }
      catch { caps[s.id] = !!_ed; } /* pure-JS fallback still present */
      continue;
    }
    caps[s.id] = !!(crypto && crypto.subtle); /* ECDSA / RSA-PSS: universal in SubtleCrypto */
  }
  return caps;
}

/* ── SEAL (v0.1.0) ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
async function sealCore(bundle, digestAlg, sigAlg, key) {
  bundle.hdr = bundle.hdr || {};
  bundle.hdr.bundle_id = bundle.hdr.bundle_id || makeId();
  bundle.hdr.spec_id = 'PROOFBUNDLE';
  bundle.hdr.spec_ver = PB_SPEC_VER;
  bundle.meta = bundle.meta || {};
  bundle.meta.created_at = bundle.meta.created_at || new Date().toISOString();
  bundle.meta.canonical_encoding = 'PB-CANON-JSON-1';
  bundle.meta.digest_alg = digestAlg;
  bundle.meta.sig_alg = sigAlg;

  const leaves = [canonicalJSON(bundle.hdr), canonicalJSON(bundle.meta), canonicalJSON(bundle.payload || {})];
  const root = await merkleRootAlg(leaves, digestAlg);
  bundle.merkleRoot = b64uEncode(root);

  const { seal: _s, sig: _l, ...forSig } = bundle;
  const digest = await digestBytes(digestAlg, canonicalJSON(forSig));
  const signature = await sigSign(sigAlg, digest, key.priv);
  bundle.seal = {
    digest_alg: digestAlg,
    digest_b64u: b64uEncode(digest),
    sig_alg: sigAlg,
    pub_b64u: key.pub,
    signature_b64u: b64uEncode(signature),
  };
  /* Degradation signal: if Ed25519 fallback is active, record it in seal metadata
     so downstream verifiers know the signing engine was pure-JS, not WebCrypto */
  if (sigAlg === 'Ed25519' && isEd25519Degraded()) {
    bundle.seal._engine = 'pure-js-fallback';
    bundle.seal._engine_note = ed25519DegradationReason();
  }
  return bundle;
}

/* ── VERIFY CORE — full outcome taxonomy ───────────────────────────
   Outcomes: VERIFIED · INVALID-SIGNATURE · MALFORMED · UNKNOWN-VERSION
   EXPIRED · RESOURCE-EXHAUSTED · LINEAGE-INVALID · POLICY-DENIED
   OUT-OF-BOUNDS · MISSING-SIDE-INFO
   Mirrors the ten expected-outcome classes of the 1097-vector suite. */
async function verifyCore(bundle, opts) {
  const o = opts || {};
  const context = o.context || {};
  const bundles = o.bundles || {};
  const maxDepth = Number.isFinite(o.maxDepth) ? o.maxDepth : 0;
  const nowMs = Number.isFinite(o.now) ? o.now : Date.now();
  const trace = [];
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const step = (title, body, outcome) => trace.push({ title, body, outcome: outcome || null, ms: elapsed(t0) });
  const fail = (outcome, title, body) => { step(title, body, outcome); return { outcome, trace }; };

  /* 1 — structural */
  if (!bundle || typeof bundle !== 'object' || Array.isArray(bundle))
    return fail('MALFORMED', 'Schema check', 'bundle is not a JSON object');
  if (!bundle.hdr || typeof bundle.hdr !== 'object')
    return fail('MALFORMED', 'Schema check', 'missing hdr');
  if (!bundle.hdr.bundle_id)
    return fail('MALFORMED', 'Schema check', 'missing hdr.bundle_id');
  if (!bundle.hdr.profile)
    return fail('MALFORMED', 'Schema check', 'missing hdr.profile');
  if (!bundle.meta || typeof bundle.meta !== 'object')
    return fail('MALFORMED', 'Schema check', 'missing meta');

  /* 2 — version */
  if (!PB_KNOWN_VERS.includes(bundle.hdr.spec_ver))

        if (ok && witnessOk)

        else if (ok !== witnessOk)
          return fail('INDETERMINATE', 'Dual signature',
            `${ok ? 'primary valid, witness INVALID' : 'primary INVALID, witness valid'} — requires investigation: partial key compromise, implementation mismatch, or targeted algorithm attack`);
        else
          return fail('INVALID-SIGNATURE', 'Dual signature', 'both layers invalid');
      } else if (bundle.hdr.profile === 'PB-GENO-1') {
        return fail('POLICY-DENIED', 'Dual-signature requirement',

      } else {
        if (!ok) return fail('INVALID-SIGNATURE', 'Signature verification', `${sigAlg} signature over ${digestAlg} digest does not verify`);
        step('Signature verified', `${sigAlg} over ${digestAlg} digest ✓`);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    return fail('INVALID-SIGNATURE', 'Signature verification', 'error: ' + e.message);
  }

  const leaves = [canonicalJSON(bundle.hdr), canonicalJSON(bundle.meta), canonicalJSON(bundle.payload || {})];
  const root = b64uEncode(await merkleRootAlg(leaves, digestAlg));
  if (root !== bundle.merkleRoot)
    return fail('INVALID-SIGNATURE', 'Merkle root mismatch', `computed ${root.slice(0,16)}… ≠ stored ${String(bundle.merkleRoot).slice(0,16)}…`);
  step('Merkle root verified', `leaf hashes (${digestAlg}) match committed root ✓`);

  /* 5 — expiration */
  if (bundle.meta.expiration) {
    const exp = Date.parse(bundle.meta.expiration);
    if (!isNaN(exp) && exp < nowMs)
      return fail('EXPIRED', 'Temporal check', `expiration ${bundle.meta.expiration} is before verification time ${new Date(nowMs).toISOString()}`);
    step('Temporal check', `expiration ${bundle.meta.expiration} — within window ✓`);
  }
  if (bundle.meta && bundle.meta.not_before) {
    const nb = Date.parse(bundle.meta.not_before);
    if (!isNaN(nb) && nb > nowMs)
      return fail('EXPIRED', 'Temporal check', `not_before ${bundle.meta.not_before} is after verification time — bundle not yet valid`);
    step('Temporal check', `not_before ${bundle.meta.not_before} — window open ✓`);
  }

  /* 6 — lineage: parents must exist AND pass integrity themselves */
  const refs = bundle.meta.refs || bundle.refs || [];
  if (refs.length > 0) {
    if (maxDepth <= 0)
      return fail('RESOURCE-EXHAUSTED', 'Lineage budget', `${refs.length} parent ref(s) present; depth budget exhausted at root (max_depth=0)`);
    for (const ref of refs) {
      const pid = typeof ref === 'string' ? ref : ref.parent_id;
      if (!bundles[pid])
        return fail('LINEAGE-INVALID', 'Lineage traversal', `parent "${pid}" referenced but not provided → lineage broken`);
      const pr = await verifyCore(bundles[pid],
        { bundles, maxDepth: maxDepth - 1, now: nowMs, _integrityOnly: true });
      if (pr.outcome !== 'VERIFIED')
        return fail('LINEAGE-INVALID', 'Lineage traversal', `parent "${pid}" fails integrity: ${pr.outcome} → lineage broken`);
    }
    step('Lineage traversal', `max_depth=${maxDepth} · ${refs.length} parent(s) present and integrity-verified ✓`);
  }
  if (o._integrityOnly) {
    step('Integrity-only mode', 'boundary/policy evaluation skipped for lineage ancestor');
    return { outcome: 'VERIFIED', trace };
  }

  /* 7 — policy (REGULATED profile requires side attestations) */
  if (bundle.hdr.profile === 'PB-REGULATED-1') {
    const sa = bundle.meta.side_attestations;
    if (!sa || (Array.isArray(sa) && sa.length === 0))
      return fail('POLICY-DENIED', 'Policy check', 'profile PB-REGULATED-1 requires meta.side_attestations — none present');
    step('Policy check', 'required side attestations present ✓');
  }

  /* 8 — boundary */
  const { outcome, details } = evalBoundary(bundle.meta.boundary, Object.assign({}, context, { __now: nowMs }));
  const bodyParts = details.map(r => `${r.pred.path} (${r.pred.op}) → ${r.result}`).join(', ');
  step('Boundary evaluation', bodyParts || 'no predicates', outcome === 'VERIFIED' ? null : outcome);
  return { outcome, trace };
}


/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   LEDGER — memory for the instrument.
   · Bundle store: every seal lands in a local registry, resolvable
     as lineage parents at verify time.
   · Hash-chained action log: every seal/verify/resolve appends an
     entry linked by prev-hash; the chain head is exportable for
     external anchoring (OTS). The instrument's own actions become
     auditable — the state exports for independent verification.
   · Sealed archives: the entire state exports as a PB-ARCHIVE-1
     bundle under the same machinery as everything else.
   · Key custody: export/import keypairs with fingerprints, so
     identity survives the tab.
   ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

const PB_LS_KEY = 'proofbundle.v2.state';
function lsAvail() {
  try { localStorage.setItem('__pb_t','1'); localStorage.removeItem('__pb_t'); return true; }
  catch (e) { return false; }
}
STATE.store = STATE.store || [];   /* [{ bundle, source, addedAt }] */
STATE.chain = STATE.chain || [];   /* [{ seq, ts, action, ref, note, prev, hash }] */

function persistState() {
  if (!lsAvail()) return false;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PB_LS_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      v: 2, claims: STATE.claims, predictions: STATE.predictions,
      store: STATE.store, chain: STATE.chain,
    }));
    return true;
  } catch (e) { console.warn('persist failed:', e.message); return false; }
}
function restoreState() {
  if (!lsAvail()) return false;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PB_LS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return false;
    const s = JSON.parse(raw);
    STATE.claims = s.claims || [];
    STATE.predictions = s.predictions || [];
    STATE.store = s.store || [];
    STATE.chain = s.chain || [];
    return true;
  } catch (e) { console.warn('restore failed:', e.message); return false; }
}

/* ── optional ledger sync (off by default; local-first is always complete on its own) ──
   This never replaces localStorage or blocks any local action. If disabled, or if the
   network is unreachable, the app behaves exactly as it did with no sync code at all —
   every seal, verify, and ledger entry works fully offline regardless of this section. */
const PB_SYNC_KEY = 'proofbundle.sync.config';
STATE.sync = STATE.sync || { enabled: false, apiBase: '', authToken: '', queue: [], lastSyncAt: null };

function loadSyncConfig() {
  if (!lsAvail()) return;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PB_SYNC_KEY);
    if (raw) Object.assign(STATE.sync, JSON.parse(raw));
  } catch (e) { /* sync config is non-essential; ignore and stay offline */ }
}
function saveSyncConfig() {
  if (!lsAvail()) return;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PB_SYNC_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      enabled: STATE.sync.enabled, apiBase: STATE.sync.apiBase, authToken: STATE.sync.authToken,
      queue: STATE.sync.queue, lastSyncAt: STATE.sync.lastSyncAt,
    }));
  } catch (e) { /* non-essential */ }
}

/* Fire-and-forget push. Never throws outward, never awaited by callers that don't
   explicitly want to wait — local operations never depend on this succeeding. */
async function syncPush(action, ref, note) {
  if (!STATE.sync.enabled || !STATE.sync.apiBase) return;
  const entry = { action, ref: ref || null, note: note || null, client_ts: new Date().toISOString() };
}
function dlStored(i) {
  const b = STATE.store[i].bundle;
  dlText(b.hdr.bundle_id + '.pb.json', JSON.stringify(b, null, 2));
}

/* ── sealed archives ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
async function buildArchiveBundle(state, digestAlg, sigAlg, key) {
  const bundle = {
    hdr: { bundle_id: makeId(), profile: 'PB-ARCHIVE-1', spec_id: 'PROOFBUNDLE', spec_ver: PB_SPEC_VER },
    meta: { created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
            archive: { claims: state.claims.length, predictions: state.predictions.length,
                       bundles: state.store.length, chain: state.chain.length } },
    payload: { state: { claims: state.claims, predictions: state.predictions,
                        store: state.store, chain: state.chain } },
  };
  await sealCore(bundle, digestAlg, sigAlg, key);
  return bundle;
}
async function exportArchive() {
  try {
    if (!_currentKey) _currentKey = await sigKeygen('Ed25519');
    const b = await buildArchiveBundle(STATE, 'SHA3-256', _currentKey.alg, _currentKey);
    await chainAppend('export-archive', b.hdr.bundle_id,
      `${STATE.store.length} bundles · ${STATE.chain.length} chain entries`);
    dlText(b.hdr.bundle_id + '.archive.pb.json', JSON.stringify(b, null, 2));
  } catch (e) { notifyError('Archive export failed: ' + e.message); }
}
async function importArchive() {
  const box = document.getElementById('ledger-import-result');
  box.style.display = 'block';
  let b;
  try {
    const fin = document.getElementById('ledger-import-file');
    if (fin.files && fin.files[0]) b = JSON.parse(await fin.files[0].text());
    else b = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('ledger-import-json').value);
  } catch (e) { box.innerHTML = errBox('Archive parse error: ' + e.message); return; }
  const { outcome } = await verifyCore(b, {});
  if (outcome !== 'VERIFIED') { box.innerHTML = errBox('Archive seal did not verify: ' + outcome + ' — refusing to restore.'); return; }
  if (b.hdr.profile !== 'PB-ARCHIVE-1' || !b.payload || !b.payload.state) {
    box.innerHTML = errBox('Verified bundle is not a PB-ARCHIVE-1 archive.'); return; }
  const cv = await verifyChain(b.payload.state.chain || []);
  const st = b.payload.state;
  STATE.claims = st.claims || []; STATE.predictions = st.predictions || [];
  STATE.store = st.store || []; STATE.chain = st.chain || [];
  await chainAppend('import-archive', b.hdr.bundle_id,
    `restored ${STATE.store.length} bundles · imported chain ${cv.ok ? 'intact' : 'BROKEN at ' + cv.brokenAt}`);
  persistState();
  renderClaims(); renderPredictions(); renderLedgerPanel();
  box.innerHTML = `<div class="vec-run-tally ${cv.ok ? 'good' : 'bad'}"><b>ARCHIVE RESTORED</b> — seal VERIFIED · ${STATE.store.length} bundles · ${STATE.claims.length} claims · ${STATE.predictions.length} predictions · imported chain ${cv.ok ? 'intact ✓' : '<b>BROKEN at entry ' + cv.brokenAt + '</b>'}</div>`;
}

/* ── key custody ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
async function keyFingerprint(pubB64u) {
  return hexOf(await digestBytes('SHA-256', b64uDecode(pubB64u))).slice(0, 16);
}
async function exportKey() {
  if (!_currentKey) { notifyError('No key in this tab — generate one first.'); return; }
  const fp = await keyFingerprint(_currentKey.pub);
  dlText(`pb-key-${_currentKey.alg}-${fp}.json`, JSON.stringify({
    kind: 'proofbundle-keypair', v: 1, alg: _currentKey.alg,
    priv_b64u: _currentKey.priv, pub_b64u: _currentKey.pub,
    fingerprint: fp, exported_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    warning: 'PRIVATE KEY MATERIAL — custody is yours',
  }, null, 2));
  await chainAppend('export-key', fp, _currentKey.alg);
}
async function importKeyFile(input) {
  try {
    const k = JSON.parse(await input.files[0].text());
    if (k.kind !== 'proofbundle-keypair' || !k.alg || !k.priv_b64u || !k.pub_b64u)
      throw new Error('not a proofbundle keypair file');
    if (!PB_SIGS.some(s => s.id === k.alg)) throw new Error('unknown algorithm ' + k.alg);
    _currentKey = { alg: k.alg, priv: k.priv_b64u, pub: k.pub_b64u };
    const fp = await keyFingerprint(k.pub_b64u);
    const sel = document.getElementById('seal-sigalg'); if (sel) sel.value = k.alg;
    const pk = document.getElementById('seal-privkey'); if (pk) pk.value = k.priv_b64u;
    const po = document.getElementById('seal-pubkey-out'); if (po) po.innerText = k.pub_b64u;
    const kd = document.getElementById('seal-keypair-display'); if (kd) kd.style.display = 'block';
    const fpEl = document.getElementById('seal-key-fp'); if (fpEl) fpEl.innerText = 'fingerprint ' + fp;
    await chainAppend('import-key', fp, k.alg);
    notifyOk(`Key loaded: ${k.alg} · fingerprint ${fp}`);
  } catch (e) { notifyError('Key import failed: ' + e.message); }
  input.value = '';
}

/* ── verification certificate ───────────────────────────────────── */
async function buildVerifyReport(subject, outcome, trace, digestAlg, sigAlg, key, nowMs) {
  const report = {
    hdr: { bundle_id: makeId(), profile: 'PB-VERIFY-REPORT-1', spec_id: 'PROOFBUNDLE', spec_ver: PB_SPEC_VER },
    meta: { created_at: new Date().toISOString() },
    payload: {
      subject_bundle_id: (subject.hdr && subject.hdr.bundle_id) || null,
      subject_root: subject.merkleRoot || null,
      subject_profile: (subject.hdr && subject.hdr.profile) || null,
      outcome, verified_at: new Date(nowMs || Date.now()).toISOString(),
      verifier_key_fingerprint: await keyFingerprint(key.pub),
      trace: trace.map(t => ({ step: t.step, detail: t.detail })),
    },
  };
  await sealCore(report, digestAlg, sigAlg, key);
  return report;
}
async function downloadVerifyReport() {
  const L = window._lastVerify;
  if (!L) { notifyError('Run a verification first.'); return; }
  try {
    if (!_currentKey) _currentKey = await sigKeygen('Ed25519');
    const r = await buildVerifyReport(L.bundle, L.outcome, L.trace, 'SHA3-256', _currentKey.alg, _currentKey, L.now);
    await registerBundle(r, 'verify-report');
    dlText(r.hdr.bundle_id + '.report.pb.json', JSON.stringify(r, null, 2));
  } catch (e) { notifyError('Report failed: ' + e.message); }
}

async function renderLedgerPanel() {

async function testDigest(id, btn) {
  btn.disabled = true; const t0 = performance.now();
  try {
    const h = await digestBytes(id, utf8('proofbundle.org'));
    btn.outerHTML = `<span class="algo-live mono" title="${hexOf(h)}">✓ ${hexOf(h).slice(0,12)}… ${Math.round(performance.now()-t0)}ms</span>`;
  } catch (e) { btn.outerHTML = `<span class="algo-live" style="color:var(--red)">✗ ${esc(e.message)}</span>`; }
}
async function testSig(id, btn) {
  btn.disabled = true; btn.innerText = '…'; const t0 = performance.now();
  try {
    const key = await sigKeygen(id);
    const msg = utf8('proofbundle signature self-test');
    const sig = await sigSign(id, msg, key.priv);
    const ok = await sigVerify(id, msg, sig, key.pub);
    const bad = sig.slice(); bad[0] ^= 0xff;
    const tamper = await sigVerify(id, msg, bad, key.pub);
    const good = ok && !tamper;
    btn.outerHTML = `<span class="algo-live" style="color:var(--${good?'green':'red'})">${good?'✓':'✗'} sign/verify/tamper ${Math.round(performance.now()-t0)}ms · sig ${sig.length}B</span>`;
  } catch (e) { btn.outerHTML = `<span class="algo-live" style="color:var(--red)">✗ ${esc(e.message)}</span>`; }
}

/* ── v2 SEAL / VERIFY UI (replacements) ─────────────────────────── */
async function generateKeyPair() {
  const alg = document.getElementById('seal-sigalg').value;
  const btn = document.getElementById('seal-keygen-btn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.innerText = 'Generating…';
  try {
    _currentKey = await sigKeygen(alg);
    document.getElementById('seal-privkey').value = _currentKey.priv;
    document.getElementById('seal-pubkey-out').innerText = _currentKey.pub;
    const fpEl = document.getElementById('seal-key-fp');
    if (fpEl) fpEl.innerText = 'fingerprint ' + await keyFingerprint(_currentKey.pub);
    document.getElementById('seal-keypair-display').style.display = 'block';
  } catch (e) { notifyError('Key generation failed: ' + e.message); }
  finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.innerText = 'Generate Key Pair'; }
}

async function sealBundle() {
  let bundle;
  try { bundle = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('seal-bundle-json').value); }
  catch (e) { notifyError('Bundle JSON is invalid: ' + e.message); return; }

  const digestAlg = document.getElementById('seal-digestalg').value;
  const sigAlg = document.getElementById('seal-sigalg').value;
  const privInput = document.getElementById('seal-privkey').value.trim();
  let key;
  if (_currentKey && _currentKey.alg === sigAlg && (!privInput || privInput === _currentKey.priv)) {
    key = _currentKey;
  } else if (privInput) {
    /* derive pub where possible; Ed25519 seed → pub; others require generated pair */
    if (sigAlg === 'Ed25519') {
      const seed = b64uDecode(privInput);
      const pub = await _ed.pubFromSeed(seed);
      key = { alg: sigAlg, priv: privInput, pub: b64uEncode(pub) };
    } else if (sigInfo(sigAlg).pbx) {
      const sk = b64uDecode(privInput);
      key = { alg: sigAlg, priv: privInput, pub: b64uEncode(PBX[sigInfo(sigAlg).pbx].getPublicKey(sk)) };
    } else {
      notifyError('For ' + sigAlg + ', generate the key pair here — click Generate Key Pair.');
      return;
    }
  } else { notifyError('Generate or paste a private key first.'); return; }

  bundle.hdr = bundle.hdr || {};
  bundle.hdr.bundle_id = makeId();
  bundle.meta = bundle.meta || {};
  bundle.meta.created_at = new Date().toISOString();
  const parentEl = document.getElementById('seal-parent');
  const parentId = parentEl ? parentEl.value.trim() : '';
  if (parentId) bundle.meta.refs = [{ parent_id: parentId }];
  try {
    await sealCore(bundle, digestAlg, sigAlg, key);
  } catch (e) { notifyError('Seal failed: ' + e.message); return; }
  await registerBundle(bundle, 'seal');
  const fpEl = document.getElementById('seal-key-fp');
  if (fpEl) fpEl.innerText = 'fingerprint ' + await keyFingerprint(key.pub);

  document.getElementById('seal-out-id').innerText = bundle.hdr.bundle_id;
  document.getElementById('seal-out-root').innerText = bundle.merkleRoot + '  (' + digestAlg + ')';
  document.getElementById('seal-output-json').innerText = JSON.stringify(bundle, null, 2);
  window._lastSealed = bundle;
  document.getElementById('seal-result').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('seal-pubkey-out').innerText = key.pub;
  document.getElementById('seal-keypair-display').style.display = 'block';
}

async function verifyBundle() {
  const resultDiv = document.getElementById('verify-result');
  resultDiv.style.display = 'block';
  resultDiv.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--text2);font-size:13px">Verifying…</div>';

  let bundle, context, bundles;
  const rawBundleText = document.getElementById('verify-bundle-json').value.trim();
  if (!rawBundleText) { resultDiv.innerHTML = errBox("There's nothing to verify yet — drop a receipt file above, or paste one in the technical box."); return; }
  try { bundle = JSON.parse(rawBundleText); }
  catch (e) { resultDiv.innerHTML = errBox("This doesn't look like a valid ProofBundle receipt — the file may be incomplete or corrupted."); return; }
  try { context = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('verify-context').value || '{}'); }
  catch (e) { resultDiv.innerHTML = errBox('The context (Advanced options) is not valid JSON — check for a missing bracket or quote.'); return; }
  try { bundles = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('verify-bundles').value || '{}'); }
  catch (e) { resultDiv.innerHTML = errBox('The provided-bundles field (Advanced options) is not valid JSON — check for a missing bracket or quote.'); return; }

  const maxDepth = parseInt(document.getElementById('verify-maxdepth').value) || 0;
  const pubOverride = document.getElementById('verify-pubkey').value.trim() || undefined;
  const asofRaw = (document.getElementById('verify-asof') || { value: '' }).value.trim();
  const asof = asofRaw ? Date.parse(asofRaw) : NaN;
  if (asofRaw && isNaN(asof)) { resultDiv.innerHTML = errBox('Verify-as-of is not a parseable timestamp: ' + asofRaw); return; }

  const useStore = (document.getElementById('verify-usestore') || { checked: false }).checked;
  const bundlesAll = useStore ? Object.assign(storeMap(), bundles) : bundles;
  const depth = useStore && maxDepth === 0 ? 8 : maxDepth;

  const nowOpt = isNaN(asof) ? undefined : asof;
  const { outcome, trace } = await verifyCore(bundle, { context, bundles: bundlesAll,
    maxDepth: depth, pubOverride, now: nowOpt });
  window._lastVerify = { bundle, outcome, trace, now: nowOpt || Date.now() };
  await chainAppend('verify', (bundle.hdr && bundle.hdr.bundle_id) || '(no id)', outcome);
  resultDiv.innerHTML = renderVerifyResult(outcome, trace) +
    `<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="downloadVerifyReport()">↓ Signed verification report</button></div>`;
}

/* ── extended outcome → style maps (replacements) ───────────────── */
function outcomeClass(o) {
  const m = {
    'VERIFIED':'verified', 'OUT-OF-BOUNDS':'oob', 'INDETERMINATE':'indet',
    'MISSING-SIDE-INFO':'indet', 'INVALID-SIGNATURE':'invsig',
    'RESOURCE-EXHAUSTED':'resex', 'INVALID':'invalid', 'MALFORMED':'invalid',
    'EXPIRED':'indet', 'POLICY-DENIED':'oob', 'UNKNOWN-VERSION':'resex',
    'LINEAGE-INVALID':'invalid',
  };
  return m[o] || 'invalid';
}
function outcomeBadgeClass(o) {
  const m = {
    'VERIFIED':'badge-green', 'OUT-OF-BOUNDS':'badge-purple',
    'INDETERMINATE':'badge-orange', 'MISSING-SIDE-INFO':'badge-orange',
    'INVALID-SIGNATURE':'badge-red', 'RESOURCE-EXHAUSTED':'badge-yellow',
    'INVALID':'badge-red', 'MALFORMED':'badge-red', 'EXPIRED':'badge-orange',
    'POLICY-DENIED':'badge-purple', 'UNKNOWN-VERSION':'badge-yellow',
    'LINEAGE-INVALID':'badge-red',
  };
  return m[String(o).toUpperCase()] || 'badge-red';
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BOOT SELF-TEST — closure within stated precision, at every boot
   ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
async function runSelfTests() {
  const R = [];
  const T = (name, ok, note) => R.push({ name, ok: !!ok, note: note || '' });
  const eq = (a, b) => a === b;

  try {
    /* encoding + canonical form */
    T('b64u round-trip', eq(b64uEncode(b64uDecode('QUJD')), 'QUJD'));
    T('canonical JSON key ordering', eq(canonicalJSON({ b:1, a:{ d:2, c:3 } }), '{"a":{"c":3,"d":2},"b":1}'));

    /* digest KATs — official vectors */
    const kat = async (alg, input, want) => T(`KAT ${alg}(${JSON.stringify(input)})`, eq(hexOf(await digestBytes(alg, utf8(input))), want));
    await kat('SHA-256', 'abc', 'ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad');

function renderVerifyResult(finalOutcome, trace) {
  const cls = outcomeClass(finalOutcome);
  let html = `<div class="verify-result ${cls}">`;
  html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px">
    <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:700">Verification Result</span>
    <span class="badge ${outcomeBadgeClass(finalOutcome)}">${finalOutcome}</span>
  </div>`;
  html += `<div class="trace-label">Execution Trace</div>`;
  for (const step of trace) {
    if (step.outcome) {
      const sc = outcomeClass(step.outcome);
      html += `<div class="trace-step outcome ${sc}">
        <div>
          <div class="trace-step-title">${esc(step.title)}</div>
          <div class="trace-step-body">${esc(step.body)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="trace-step-right">
          <span class="badge ${outcomeBadgeClass(step.outcome)}">${step.outcome}</span>
          <span class="trace-ms">${step.ms}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    } else {
      html += `<div class="trace-step">
        <div class="trace-step-title">${esc(step.title)}</div>
        <div class="trace-step-body">${esc(step.body)}</div>
      </div>`;
    }
  }
  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}

function errBox(msg) {
  return `<div class="verify-result invsig"><span style="color:var(--red)">${esc(msg)}</span></div>`;
}

function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
}

/* ── hash-chained action log ────────────────────────────────────── */
async function chainAppend(action, ref, note) {
  const prev = STATE.chain.length ? STATE.chain[STATE.chain.length - 1].hash : 'genesis';
  const entry = { seq: STATE.chain.length, ts: new Date().toISOString(),
                  action, ref: ref || null, note: note || null, prev };
  entry.hash = b64uEncode(await digestBytes('SHA-256', utf8(canonicalJSON(entry))));
  STATE.chain.push(entry);
  persistState();
  syncPush(action, ref, note); /* best-effort, non-blocking, entirely optional */
  return entry;
}
async function verifyChain(chain) {
  const c = chain || STATE.chain;
  for (let i = 0; i < c.length; i++) {
    const { hash, ...rest } = c[i];
    const want = b64uEncode(await digestBytes('SHA-256', utf8(canonicalJSON(rest))));
    if (want !== hash) return { ok: false, brokenAt: i, why: 'entry hash mismatch' };
    const expPrev = i === 0 ? 'genesis' : c[i-1].hash;
    if (rest.prev !== expPrev) return { ok: false, brokenAt: i, why: 'prev-link mismatch' };
  }
  return { ok: true, length: c.length, head: c.length ? c[c.length-1].hash : 'genesis' };
}

/* ── bundle store ───────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function storeMap() {
  const m = {};
  for (const e of STATE.store) if (e.bundle && e.bundle.hdr) m[e.bundle.hdr.bundle_id] = e.bundle;
  return m;
}
async function registerBundle(bundle, source) {
  if (!bundle || !bundle.hdr || !bundle.hdr.bundle_id) return;
  const id = bundle.hdr.bundle_id;
  if (!STATE.store.some(e => e.bundle.hdr.bundle_id === id))
    STATE.store.push({ bundle, source: source || 'seal', addedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
  await chainAppend(source || 'seal', id,
    `${bundle.hdr.profile || '?'} · ${(bundle.seal && bundle.seal.sig_alg) || (bundle.sig && bundle.sig.alg) || '?'}`);
  persistState();
  renderLedgerPanel();
}
function removeStored(i) { STATE.store.splice(i, 1); persistState(); renderLedgerPanel(); }
function useAsParent(i) {
  const id = STATE.store[i].bundle.hdr.bundle_id;
  const el = document.getElementById('seal-parent');
  if (el) {
    el.value = id;
    switchTab('seal', document.querySelector('.sb-nav button[data-title="Bundle editor"]'));
    flashToast('Bundle set as parent in the Bundle editor.');
  }
}

  const e = STATE.store[i];

  await chainAppend('verify', e.bundle.hdr.bundle_id, outcome);
  const cell = document.getElementById('store-oc-' + i);
  if (cell) cell.innerHTML = `<span class="badge ${outcomeBadgeClass(outcome)}">${outcome}</span>`;
}

async function refreshAgentLineage() {
  const box=document.getElementById('agent-live'); if(!box)return;
  try {
    const res=await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:8788/lineage/export',{cache:'no-store'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('bridge HTTP '+res.status);
    const data=await res.json(), entries=Array.isArray(data.entries)?data.entries:[];
    let previous='genesis', valid=0, failure=null;
    for(let i=0;i<entries.length;i++){
      const e=entries[i];
      if(e.seq!==i||e.prev_hash!==previous){failure=`chain mismatch at ${i}`;break;}
      if(!e.payload?.encrypted){
        const bodyHash=hexOf(await digestBytes('SHA-256',utf8(canonicalJSON(e.payload?.body))));
        if(bodyHash!==e.payload?.plaintext_hash){failure=`payload hash mismatch at ${i}`;break;}
      }
      const unsigned=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(e)); unsigned.signature=null;
      const pubRaw=h2b(String(e.from?.pubkey||'').slice(-64));
      const sig=h2b(String(e.signature||''));
      if(pubRaw.length!==32||sig.length!==64||!(await _ed.verify(utf8(canonicalJSON(unsigned)),sig,pubRaw))){failure=`signature mismatch at ${i}`;break;}
      previous=hexOf(await digestBytes('SHA-256',utf8(canonicalJSON(e)))); valid++;
    }
    STATE.agentReceipts=entries; persistState();
    const rows=entries.slice(-20).reverse().map(e=>`<tr><td>${e.seq}</td><td>${esc(e.from?.agent_id||'?')}</td><td>${esc(e.payload_type||'?')}</td><td class="mono">${esc((e.payload?.plaintext_hash||'').slice(0,18))}…</td></tr>`).join('');
    box.innerHTML=`<span class="badge ${failure?'badge-red':'badge-green'}">${failure?esc(failure):'verified'}</span> ${valid}/${entries.length} signatures + payload hashes + chain links verified locally`+(rows?`<table class="mini-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>agent</th><th>type</th><th>payload hash</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`:'');
  } catch(e) { box.innerHTML=`<span class="badge badge-orange">offline</span> ${esc(e.message)} — ingestion retries automatically`; }
}

function cartesian(a, b) { const out=[]; for (const x of a) for (const y of b) out.push([x,y]); return out; }

const PB_RUN_KEYCACHE = {};
async function runnerKey(sigAlg) {
  if (!PB_RUN_KEYCACHE[sigAlg]) PB_RUN_KEYCACHE[sigAlg] = await sigKeygen(sigAlg);
  return PB_RUN_KEYCACHE[sigAlg];
}

function runnerBaseBundle(profile) {
  return {
    hdr: { profile: profile || 'PB-BOUNDARY-1' },
    meta: {
      producer_id: 'urn:pb:conformance-runner',
      boundary: { all: [
        { op:'equals',  path:'env', value:'production' },
        { op:'present', path:'side_token' },
      ]},
      expiration: new Date(Date.now() + 3600e3).toISOString(),
    },
    payload: { note: 'live conformance case' },
  };
}
const RUNNER_CTX_OK = { env:'production', side_token:'tok-1' };

/* The ten outcome classes of the frozen suite, rebuilt live. */
async function buildRunnerCases(digestAlg, sigAlg) {
  const key = await runnerKey(sigAlg);
  const seal = (b) => sealCore(b, digestAlg, sigAlg, key);
  const clone = (b) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(b));

  const main = await seal(runnerBaseBundle());

  const withRefs = runnerBaseBundle();
  withRefs.meta.refs = [{ parent_id: 'pb-parent-absent' }];
  const refsSealed = await seal(withRefs);

  const regulated = runnerBaseBundle('PB-REGULATED-1');
  const regulatedSealed = await seal(regulated);

  const expired = runnerBaseBundle();
  expired.meta.expiration = new Date(Date.now() - 3600e3).toISOString();
  const expiredSealed = await seal(expired);

  const tampered = clone(main);
  const sigStr = tampered.seal.signature_b64u;
  tampered.seal.signature_b64u = (sigStr[0] === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A') + sigStr.slice(1);

  const malformed = clone(main); delete malformed.seal;
  const badver = clone(main); badver.hdr.spec_ver = '99.0.0';

  return [
    { expected:'VERIFIED',           bundle: main,            opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
    { expected:'INVALID-SIGNATURE',  bundle: tampered,        opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
    { expected:'MALFORMED',          bundle: malformed,       opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
    { expected:'UNKNOWN-VERSION',    bundle: badver,          opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
    { expected:'EXPIRED',            bundle: expiredSealed,   opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
    { expected:'OUT-OF-BOUNDS',      bundle: clone(main),     opts:{ context: { env:'staging', side_token:'tok-1' } } },
    { expected:'MISSING-SIDE-INFO',  bundle: clone(main),     opts:{ context: { env:'production' } } },
    { expected:'RESOURCE-EXHAUSTED', bundle: refsSealed,      opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK, maxDepth: 0 } },
    { expected:'LINEAGE-INVALID',    bundle: clone(refsSealed), opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK, maxDepth: 2, bundles: {} } },
    { expected:'POLICY-DENIED',      bundle: regulatedSealed, opts:{ context: RUNNER_CTX_OK } },
  ];
}

async function runConformance() {
  const scopeId = document.getElementById('vec-run-scope').value;
  const scope = PB_RUN_SCOPES[scopeId];
  const out = document.getElementById('vec-run-result');
  const btn = document.getElementById('vec-run-btn');
  btn.disabled = true;
  out.style.display = 'block';
  const t0 = performance.now();
  let pass = 0, fail = 0;
  const failures = [];
  const cells = {};
  try {
    for (let i = 0; i < scope.pairs.length; i++) {
      const [d, s] = scope.pairs[i];
      out.innerHTML = `<div class="vec-running">Running ${esc(scope.label)} — pair ${i+1}/${scope.pairs.length}: ${esc(d)} × ${esc(s)} · ${pass} pass / ${fail} fail</div>`;
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 0)); /* let UI paint */
      let cases;
      try { cases = await buildRunnerCases(d, s); }
      catch (e) { fail += 10; failures.push({ d, s, case:'(seal)', err: e.message }); cells[d+'|'+s] = '0/10'; continue; }
      let p = 0;
      for (const c of cases) {
        const { outcome } = await verifyCore(c.bundle, c.opts);
        if (outcome === c.expected) { pass++; p++; }
        else { fail++; failures.push({ d, s, case: c.expected, got: outcome }); }
      }
      cells[d+'|'+s] = p + '/10';
    }
  } finally { btn.disabled = false; }
  const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
  const digs = [...new Set(scope.pairs.map(p=>p[0]))];
  const sgs  = [...new Set(scope.pairs.map(p=>p[1]))];
  let html = `<div class="vec-run-tally ${fail? 'bad':'good'}">` +
    `<b>${pass}</b> pass · <b>${fail}</b> fail · ${scope.pairs.length*10} live cases · ${ms}ms — engine running in <i>this</i> browser</div>`;
  html += `<table class="mini-table"><thead><tr><th>digest \\ sig</th>${sgs.map(s=>`<th>${esc(s)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>` +
    digs.map(d => `<tr><td><b>${esc(d)}</b></td>${sgs.map(s=>{
      const v = cells[d+'|'+s] || '—';
      return `<td class="${v==='10/10'?'cell-good':'cell-bad'}">${v}</td>`;}).join('')}</tr>`).join('') +
    `</tbody></table>`;
  if (failures.length) {
    html += `<div class="vec-failures"><b>Failures</b><br>` + failures.slice(0,30).map(f =>
      `${esc(f.d)} × ${esc(f.s)} · ${esc(f.case)} → ${esc(f.got || f.err)}`).join('<br>') + `</div>`;
  }
  out.innerHTML = html;
}

/* ── IMPORT RUNNER — full-payload conformance-vectors.json ───────── */
async function importVectorsRun() {

import asyncio, json, sys
from playwright.async_api import async_playwright

URL = sys.argv[1]
async def main():
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        b = await p.chromium.launch()
        pg = await b.new_page()
        errs = []

        # full live conformance matrix, all digests x all sig algs
        res = await pg.evaluate("""async () => {
          let pass=0, fail=0; const bad=[];
          for (const d of PB_DIGESTS) for (const s of PB_SIGS) {
            let cases;
            try { cases = await buildRunnerCases(d.id, s.id); }
            catch (e) { fail += 10; bad.push(d.id+'x'+s.id+' seal: '+e.message); continue; }
            for (const c of cases) {
              const { outcome } = await verifyCore(c.bundle, c.opts);
              if (outcome === c.expected) pass++; else { fail++; bad.push(d.id+'x'+s.id+' '+c.expected+'->'+outcome); }
            }
          }
          return { pass, fail, bad: bad.slice(0,20) };
        }""")
        print("CONFORMANCE", json.dumps(res))
        # verify-report round-trip through JSON (what a downloaded report goes through)
        rep = await pg.evaluate("""async () => {
          const key = await sigKeygen('Ed25519');
          const subj = await sealCore(runnerBaseBundle(), 'SHA3-256', 'Ed25519', key);
          const v = await verifyCore(subj, { context: RUNNER_CTX_OK });
          const r = await buildVerifyReport(subj, v.outcome, v.trace, 'SHA3-256', 'Ed25519', key, Date.now());
          const inMem = (await verifyCore(r, {})).outcome;
          const viaJson = (await verifyCore(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r)), {})).outcome;
          return { inMem, viaJson, firstTraceEntry: JSON.stringify(r.payload.trace[0]), traceKeys: Object.keys(v.trace[0]) };
        }""")
        print("VERIFY-REPORT", json.dumps(rep))
        print("ERRORS", len(errs))
        for e in errs[:15]: print("  ", e[:200])
        await b.close()
asyncio.run(main())
EOF

function dlText(name, text, mime) {
  const blob = new Blob([text], { type: mime || 'application/json' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); a.download = name;
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
  setTimeout(() => { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 800);
}

function filesDropInit() {
  const dz = document.getElementById('files-drop');
  if (!dz) return;
  ['dragover','dragenter'].forEach(ev => dz.addEventListener(ev, e => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add('drop-hot'); }));
function flashToast(msg, kind) {
  let t = document.getElementById('pb-toast');
  if (!t) {
    t = document.createElement('div');
    t.id = 'pb-toast';
    t.className = 'pb-toast';
    document.body.appendChild(t);
  }
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.className = 'pb-toast show' + (kind ? ' pb-toast-' + kind : '');
  clearTimeout(t._hideTimer);
  t._hideTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 4200);
}
